[u0005 | 00:00:05.640-00:00:11.780 | DM] the color of the desert, however, pales in comparison to the vividness of your nightly dreams.
[u0006 | 00:00:11.780-00:00:12.080 | DM] Um,
[u0007 | 00:00:16.980-00:00:20.160 | DM] something seems to have changed since you left Tokra.
[u0008 | 00:00:20.160-00:00:26.220 | DM] In the past, these dreams are rare, maybe a few times a year, but now every night takes you to a new landscape.
[u0009 | 00:00:27.440-00:00:29.640 | DM] Some of them are familiar from your childhood.
[u0010 | 00:00:29.640-00:00:35.180 | DM] A lush forest, in autumn, leaves turning golden, high peaks surrounding you.
[u0011 | 00:00:35.180-00:00:40.900 | DM] An island in the middle of an ocean with nothing but a single dark, tall tower standing on it.
[u0012 | 00:00:41.910-00:00:48.339 | DM] A tall, green hill, perfectly round, with small white flowers growing on it, surrounded by desert.
[u0013 | 00:00:49.400-00:00:52.900 | DM] Others are new and just flashes quicker than you can keep track of.
[u0014 | 00:00:52.900-00:00:59.280 | DM] A boat racked by waves, s- un- surprisingly to you, ma- seemingly made of stone.
[u0015 | 00:00:59.280-00:01:05.080 | DM] An impossible castle floating in the air above an expansive patchwork of small farms and cities.
[u0016 | 00:01:05.080-00:01:09.080 | DM] A vast, endless, expansive forest as far as the eye can see.
[u0017 | 00:01:09.080-00:01:11.440 | DM] But there's one that keeps returning night after night.
[u0018 | 00:01:11.440-00:01:17.800 | DM] A red sandstone mesa in a rocky desert, a small, clear oasis sheltering in the shadow of its cliffs.
[u0019 | 00:01:17.800-00:01:19.060 | Kenzo] Say that one again.
[u0020 | 00:01:19.540-00:01:27.100 | DM] A l- a red sandstone mesa in the, in a rocky desert, a small, clear oasis sheltering in the shadow of its cliffs.
[u0021 | 00:01:30.120-00:01:37.760 | DM] As you approach Karawa, the first thing you see from a distance in the morning light is a red sandstone mesa standing above the rocky desert ground.
[u0022 | 00:01:39.480-00:01:46.820 | DM] The desert flowers grow thickly here, scrubby, thorny plants bursting in color in the late spring before the heat of summer dries up the ground.
[u0023 | 00:01:46.820-00:01:53.180 | DM] But you hardly notice as your steps slow, then stop, and you realize that you are staring at an image out of your dreams.
[u0024 | 00:01:54.660-00:01:58.380 | DM] A temple is carved into the side of the mesa, and extends from it.
[u0025 | 00:01:58.380-00:02:03.039 | DM] The oasis is enclosed in a stone pool and surrounded by swaying palms.
[u0026 | 00:02:03.040-00:02:13.040 | DM] There's a small village that seems to have sprung up around, of mud houses and narrow streets, punctured by a few larger buildings and a large sandstone plaza.
[u0027 | 00:02:13.040-00:02:16.320 | DM] All of this has grown up from the desert of your dreams.
[u0028 | 00:02:16.320-00:02:21.660 | DM] A bustling tavern caravanserai stands on the outskirts of town.
[u0029 | 00:02:21.660-00:02:27.800 | DM] But take away the buildings, and the scene in front of you is unmistakably one you have seen many times over the past week of travel.
[u0030 | 00:02:29.860-00:02:40.180 | DM] Unsure of what it might mean, but drawn by an immediate connection to this place that seems at once deeply familiar and completely new, you start to head down in towards town,
[u0031 | 00:02:40.180-00:02:45.080 | DM] uh, heading down, uh, towards the caravanserai to find a room for the night,
[u0032 | 00:02:47.020-00:02:49.020 | DM] thinking about what this might mean.
[u0033 | 00:02:50.100-00:02:59.880 | DM] As you approach, you hear f- ahead of you, shouts and cries of help, breaking your reverie and snapping your attention to something that seems quite far ahead.
[u0034 | 00:02:59.880-00:03:07.390 | DM] A giant beast, two of them, hyena-like in form, but much larger than any hyena you've seen, charging
[u0035 | 00:03:08.940-00:03:11.410 | DM] into the village, people shouting,
[u0036 | 00:03:12.820-00:03:19.320 | DM] and cries as the villagers scatter in the face of these, uh, beasts appearing.
[u0037 | 00:03:21.880-00:03:22.880 | DM] And I'm gonna leave you there.

[u0080 | 00:05:02.880-00:05:07.360 | DM] In the past few months, you've settled into a routine in the village.
[u0081 | 00:05:07.360-00:05:17.800 | DM] After the Dunmari got over the shock of seeing a real elf in their midst, probably the first one many of them had ever seen in their lives, and learned you could be useful around the village, you found lodging in the top of a small,
[u0082 | 00:05:17.800-00:05:22.560 | DM] top floor of a small house above the tea hou- above a, a small house above a tea house,
[u0083 | 00:05:24.600-00:05:29.480 | DM] and began exploring what you could of, of these mysteries of, of Shakun.
[u0084 | 00:05:30.660-00:05:32.980 | DM] The village is small, but seems to suit your needs for now.
[u0085 | 00:05:34.440-00:05:43.320 | DM] The oasis, surrounded by a short marble wall, is the center of town, and the shade of the swaying palms dotting the plaza around it is a pleasant place to pass the time out of the heat.
[u0086 | 00:05:44.500-00:05:54.960 | DM] Surrounding the oasis, there are small clusters of mud houses and narrow streets that stretch in three dirac- directions, while to the south stands the imposing edifice of the Temple of Shakun,
[u0087 | 00:05:54.960-00:05:58.280 | DM] partially carved into the red cliffs of the mesa to the south of town.
[u0088 | 00:05:59.600-00:06:02.260 | DM] Uh, you haven't been...
[u0089 | 00:06:02.260-00:06:12.331 | DM] You've been, uh, you know, asking around about the, the temple, and there's- A number of, like, lay acolytes and, and other sort of people
[u0090 | 00:06:12.332-00:06:15.822 | DM] associated with it who are perfectly happy to say they're associated with it.
[u0091 | 00:06:15.822-00:06:24.932 | DM] And you do occasionally meet, um, see, and have talked to a few times in the past, uh, Speaker Candrosa, who's the sort of public face, um, of the temp-
[u0092 | 00:06:24.932-00:06:24.972 | Unknown] Right.
[u0093 | 00:06:24.972-00:06:26.212 | DM] People do join.
[u0094 | 00:06:26.212-00:06:27.332 | DM] Um, there are, you know,
[u0095 | 00:06:28.692-00:06:31.692 | DM] uh, you've at least heard, although you don't have...
[u0096 | 00:06:31.692-00:06:37.492 | DM] You haven't heard firsthand from anybody of initiation ceremonies, usually a few times a year.
[u0097 | 00:06:37.492-00:06:39.311 | DM] But they're, they're secret.
[u0098 | 00:06:39.311-00:06:42.592 | DM] It's sort of like a combination of a monastery and the Freemasons.
[u0099 | 00:06:42.592-00:06:52.592 | DM] So it's not... The existence of it isn't secret, and, you know, people talk about their affiliation with it occasionally, but what actually goes along, goes on behind
[u0100 | 00:06:52.592-00:06:53.212 | DM] the-
[u0101 | 00:06:53.212-00:06:53.542 | Unknown] Right
[u0102 | 00:06:53.542-00:06:56.972 | DM] ... edifice of the, of the temple is unclear.
[u0103 | 00:06:58.232-00:07:00.892 | DM] Um, your lodging is at the edge of town,
[u0104 | 00:07:02.312-00:07:12.371 | DM] near the sprawling caravanserai that serves as, um, a marketplace and social center for travelers, and the place where you shop and, you know, um, a sort of trading area.
[u0105 | 00:07:12.372-00:07:22.492 | DM] During the winter months, not that many people pass through, um, because most of the Dunmar are out with their herds, um, when there's the, the rains and, and the desert is more
[u0106 | 00:07:22.492-00:07:23.792 | DM] hospitable.
[u0107 | 00:07:23.792-00:07:27.652 | DM] Um, but in the past few weeks, traffl- traffic has increased.
[u0108 | 00:07:27.652-00:07:32.032 | DM] You even heard rumors of a halfling who arrived a few days ago alone, which is unusual.
[u0109 | 00:07:33.492-00:07:36.732 | DM] However, more troubling in the past couple of weeks have been the attacks.
[u0110 | 00:07:36.732-00:07:46.892 | DM] Rarely if ever do wild animals venture into town, but twice in the past two weeks, small packs of hyenas, wild with furry, fury, have charged into town looking for
[u0111 | 00:07:46.892-00:07:47.231 | Unknown] prey.
[u0112 | 00:07:48.482-00:07:55.612 | DM] These are... The attacks are the talk of the town, and your status in the eyes of many have gone up after you helped drive off one, the hyenas in the first attack.
[u0113 | 00:07:56.672-00:08:02.222 | DM] Um, you're often known around town as just the elf, or elf, since, you know, most people have-
[u0114 | 00:08:02.222-00:08:02.222 | Unknown] Right
[u0115 | 00:08:02.222-00:08:06.292 | DM] ... never seen. You're the only elf anywhere in, in 100 miles, as far as you know.

[u1126 | 01:01:25.790-01:01:27.490 | DM] Dear, dear, dear.
[u1127 | 01:01:27.490-01:01:30.089 | DM] Investigation.
[u1128 | 01:01:32.350-01:01:33.210 | DM] Do, do, do.
[u1129 | 01:01:39.390-01:01:41.990 | DM] Okay. Is any... What's, uh...
[u1130 | 01:01:41.990-01:01:46.590 | DM] Just go around what everybody else is doing while, um, this dwarf is examining the-
[u1131 | 01:01:46.590-01:01:49.130 | Delwath] So actually, I will...
[u1132 | 01:01:49.130-01:01:52.230 | Delwath] First I wanna go investigate the body for a, just for a minute.
[u1133 | 01:01:52.230-01:01:55.170 | Delwath] Like, is it, is it natural?
[u1134 | 01:01:55.170-01:01:57.630 | Delwath] Does it seem to be, like, um,
[u1135 | 01:01:59.030-01:01:59.530 | Kenzo] a...
[u1136 | 01:02:01.310-01:02:07.050 | Delwath] Is it, like, naturally grown this big or does it seem to be enchanted or cursed or somehow magically whatever?
[u1137 | 01:02:09.330-01:02:09.610 | Unknown] Um,
[u1138 | 01:02:12.550-01:02:16.270 | DM] the... So I mean, you've heard rumor of
[u1139 | 01:02:17.990-01:02:21.230 | DM] beasts that grow this big out in the blasted plains.
[u1140 | 01:02:23.070-01:02:28.350 | DM] You, you all probably would've heard rumors of that, especially those of you who came from the east.
[u1141 | 01:02:28.350-01:02:30.450 | DM] So it's not... It wouldn't be, um,
[u1142 | 01:02:33.070-01:02:33.390 | Unknown] uh,
[u1143 | 01:02:35.390-01:02:38.830 | DM] it wouldn't be, like, something that was...
[u1144 | 01:02:38.830-01:02:42.730 | DM] It doesn't look like a normal creature that was, like, somebody cast Enlarge on or something like-
[u1145 | 01:02:42.730-01:02:43.570 | DM] Right. Right
[u1146 | 01:02:43.570-01:02:46.110 | DM] ... You wouldn't be, like, totally shocked to see something like...
[u1147 | 01:02:46.110-01:02:47.770 | DM] Although, somewhat shocked.
[u1148 | 01:02:47.770-01:02:47.780 | Unknown] [coughs]
[u1149 | 01:02:47.780-01:02:50.030 | DM] But you have heard stories about this.
[u1150 | 01:02:51.950-01:02:52.190 | Unknown] Um,
[u1151 | 01:02:54.010-01:02:54.330 | Unknown] uh-
[u1152 | 01:02:55.730-01:02:59.450 | Delwath] And after I kind of poke at the body for a minute, um,
[u1153 | 01:03:01.090-01:03:02.690 | Delwath] uh, I'm gonna go,
[u1154 | 01:03:04.190-01:03:10.370 | Delwath] um, you s- see, uh, Delwath start to, like, take a few deep breaths and kind of, um,
[u1155 | 01:03:11.650-01:03:11.680 | Unknown] uh,
[u1156 | 01:03:12.770-01:03:20.610 | Delwath] close his eyes briefly and, um, start, like, concentrating on, you know, looking like he's concentrating on something.
[u1157 | 01:03:23.550-01:03:25.750 | DM] Okay. So that's what you're doing.
[u1158 | 01:03:25.750-01:03:29.070 | DM] Seeker's investigating the bodies.
[u1159 | 01:03:29.070-01:03:30.970 | DM] Wellby and Kenzo, what do you guys?
[u1160 | 01:03:32.170-01:03:34.510 | Kenzo] So we're the only two that aren't doing anything right now?
[u1161 | 01:03:34.510-01:03:35.050 | DM] Correct.
[u1162 | 01:03:35.050-01:03:37.630 | Kenzo] [laughs] But I, I, I...
[u1163 | 01:03:37.630-01:03:40.570 | Kenzo] I look over, I look over at the, at the halfling.
[u1164 | 01:03:40.570-01:03:41.870 | Kenzo] You're, you're a halfling, right?
[u1165 | 01:03:41.870-01:03:42.170 | Unknown] Mm-hmm.
[u1166 | 01:03:43.550-01:03:46.030 | Kenzo] I find, you know, I've, I've got my sta- I'm pretty badly wounded.
[u1167 | 01:03:46.030-01:03:50.599 | Kenzo] Like, the, the hyena, like, got me really good, like, kind of slashed me right across the, gashed me across the chest.
[u1168 | 01:03:50.599-01:03:50.630 | Unknown] Yeah.
[u1169 | 01:03:50.630-01:03:52.890 | Kenzo] So, like, I, I'm definitely not looking that hot.
[u1170 | 01:03:52.890-01:03:55.870 | Kenzo] So I, I kind of, you know, walk over to, to the halfling.
[u1171 | 01:03:55.870-01:03:59.890 | Kenzo] Like, walk over to you, uh, Schwartz, kind of grimacing, and I, and I look over.
[u1172 | 01:03:59.890-01:04:05.670 | Kenzo] Did I... Did... Would I have been, would I have been able to observe, um, uh, Schwartz's character healing or doing anything with the commoner?

[u1175 | 01:04:09.840-01:04:09.840 | Unknown] Oh, I-
[u1176 | 01:04:09.840-01:04:11.510 | DM] ... bandaged him up.
[u1177 | 01:04:11.510-01:04:15.900 | Kenzo] Right. Right. So I look over at you, Schwartz, and I, and I'm like, "And, uh-"
[u1178 | 01:04:15.900-01:04:17.630 | DM] Um, Eric and, and Kong, I both...

[u1180 | 01:04:19.640-01:04:19.650 | Unknown] Okay.
[u1181 | 01:04:19.650-01:04:20.070 | Unknown] Yeah, good.
[u1182 | 01:04:20.070-01:04:21.570 | DM] I mean, on chat.
[u1183 | 01:04:23.670-01:04:23.850 | Unknown] Okay.
[u1184 | 01:04:23.850-01:04:23.930 | Unknown] Uh.
[u1185 | 01:04:23.930-01:04:24.380 | Wellby] So, uh,
[u1186 | 01:04:25.530-01:04:26.890 | Wellby] friend, that's an ugly looking one.
[u1187 | 01:04:26.890-01:04:28.819 | Wellby] I wish I could do something for you.
[u1188 | 01:04:28.819-01:04:28.830 | Unknown] [laughs]
[u1189 | 01:04:28.830-01:04:33.430 | Wellby] About the best I can do is, uh, keep your remaining blood inside.
[u1190 | 01:04:33.430-01:04:37.010 | Kenzo] [laughs] I was just gonna say, I, I saw what you did there with that, uh,
[u1191 | 01:04:38.330-01:04:39.350 | Kenzo] that, that gentleman and I...
[u1192 | 01:04:41.410-01:04:43.539 | Kenzo] If you've got some skill, skill for me, I wouldn't mind.
[u1193 | 01:04:45.310-01:04:48.870 | Wellby] I, uh, I, I wish I could help, but, um-
[u1194 | 01:04:48.870-01:04:50.910 | Kenzo] Keeping the blood inside sounds pretty good right now.
[u1195 | 01:04:50.910-01:04:52.290 | Kenzo] I'm not, I'm not doing too well.
[u1196 | 01:04:52.290-01:04:55.310 | Kenzo] That, that was a pretty, that was a pretty rough attack.
[u1197 | 01:04:55.310-01:04:56.570 | Kenzo] I don't know what those things were, but-
[u1198 | 01:05:00.230-01:05:02.150 | DM] Um, h- hold on, hold on one sec.
[u1199 | 01:05:02.150-01:05:03.949 | DM] There's too many questions coming in.
[u1200 | 01:05:04.010-01:05:05.230 | DM] You guys keep talking.
[u1201 | 01:05:05.230-01:05:06.510 | DM] Mike, hold on one sec.
[u1202 | 01:05:06.510-01:05:07.030 | Kenzo] Yeah, sure.
[u1203 | 01:05:08.110-01:05:09.110 | Wellby] So, um, uh-
[u1204 | 01:05:09.110-01:05:10.830 | Kenzo] What's your name, traveler? What's your name, traveler?
[u1205 | 01:05:10.830-01:05:12.270 | DM] Uh, Wellby Goodbarrel.
[u1206 | 01:05:12.270-01:05:12.650 | Unknown] And you?
[u1207 | 01:05:13.740-01:05:13.790 | Unknown] [laughs]
[u1208 | 01:05:13.790-01:05:16.490 | Seeker] Kenzo.
[u1209 | 01:05:18.870-01:05:21.550 | Wellby] Kenzo. Kenzo, you're good in a fight with that big stick of yours.
[u1210 | 01:05:23.770-01:05:32.409 | Kenzo] Uh, it serves me well from time to time, but I, I try not to get into tussles with, uh, beasts like that if I, if I can avoid it.
[u1211 | 01:05:32.410-01:05:32.610 | Unknown] Yeah, I, I-
[u1212 | 01:05:32.610-01:05:34.250 | Wellby] You ever seen these things before?
[u1213 | 01:05:34.250-01:05:36.050 | Wellby] I say generally to the group.
[u1214 | 01:05:36.050-01:05:36.830 | Unknown] [laughs]
[u1215 | 01:05:36.830-01:05:40.210 | Kenzo] Are we all outside now? I thought, I thought Mike was still inside with the, uh-
[u1216 | 01:05:40.210-01:05:43.610 | Delwath] No, I'm outside in, like, a trance.
[u1217 | 01:05:43.610-01:05:45.010 | Kenzo] Oh, okay. Okay.
[u1218 | 01:05:45.010-01:05:50.740 | Seeker] I, I wanted to, uh, thank both of you fighters for dispatching these, uh, nasty, uh...
[u1219 | 01:05:50.740-01:05:52.610 | Seeker] Are they more like dogs or more like cats?
[u1220 | 01:05:52.670-01:05:55.670 | Seeker] I don't know. I've been poking around at its hide.
[u1221 | 01:05:55.670-01:06:05.670 | Seeker] Uh, I, I did notice, uh, that there's quite a bit of, uh, of dried blood all over, uh, the body of this, uh, this one at least over here that's got its head in the
[u1222 | 01:06:05.670-01:06:05.890 | Unknown] hole.
[u1223 | 01:06:06.950-01:06:11.010 | Seeker] Um, I, I wonder if it's, i- if it's been, um,
[u1224 | 01:06:12.070-01:06:15.370 | Seeker] in some, in some battles on the way over here.
[u1225 | 01:06:15.370-01:06:15.500 | Unknown] Oh.
[u1226 | 01:06:15.500-01:06:17.830 | Seeker] Uh, maybe even made some kills.
[u1227 | 01:06:17.830-01:06:19.530 | Seeker] Um, I wonder if we could follow them-
[u1228 | 01:06:20.710-01:06:20.720 | Unknown] [coughs]
[u1229 | 01:06:20.720-01:06:23.910 | Seeker] ... uh, over the desert to see where they came from.
[u1230 | 01:06:23.910-01:06:23.920 | Unknown] We could track it.
[u1231 | 01:06:23.920-01:06:25.330 | DM] What's your what?
[u1232 | 01:06:25.330-01:06:26.930 | Unknown] [coughs]
[u1233 | 01:06:26.930-01:06:27.230 | Unknown] What'd you say?
[u1234 | 01:06:29.810-01:06:31.730 | Wellby] Well, I started to say we could track it back, but then...
[u1235 | 01:06:31.730-01:06:34.350 | Wellby] Oh, uh, friends, you haven't been introduced yet.
[u1236 | 01:06:35.390-01:06:37.610 | Kenzo] I said, what, what, what's your name, traveler?
[u1237 | 01:06:37.610-01:06:40.390 | Seeker] Oh, I, I am, uh, Seeker of Highkeep.
[u1238 | 01:06:43.110-01:06:43.910 | DM] Your name is Seeker?
[u1239 | 01:06:45.050-01:06:45.450 | Unknown] That's right.
[u1240 | 01:06:46.530-01:06:48.410 | Delwath] Can you turn down the music a tiny bit, Tim?
[u1241 | 01:06:48.410-01:06:49.050 | Seeker] Yeah.
[u1242 | 01:06:53.510-01:06:53.770 | Unknown] Uh,
[u1243 | 01:06:56.590-01:06:57.350 | Seeker] nice to meet you all.
[u1244 | 01:06:59.410-01:06:59.610 | Unknown] Huh.
[u1245 | 01:07:00.750-01:07:04.090 | Kenzo] Is this, uh, Seeker your, your, your given name, or-
[u1246 | 01:07:06.222-01:07:07.942 | Seeker] Uh, I chose it,
[u1247 | 01:07:09.062-01:07:09.542 | Seeker] uh-
[u1248 | 01:07:09.542-01:07:09.551 | Unknown] Uh-
[u1249 | 01:07:09.551-01:07:11.802 | Seeker] At a moment of lucidity.
[u1250 | 01:07:13.622-01:07:14.822 | Wellby] Hmm, hmm.
[u1251 | 01:07:14.822-01:07:22.662 | Seeker] Uh, I don't know if you're familiar with, uh, dwarven culture, but we have a, uh, rite of passage, uh, ritual in adolescence.
[u1252 | 01:07:22.662-01:07:22.882 | Unknown] Uh,
[u1253 | 01:07:24.862-01:07:25.482 | Seeker] it's uh-
[u1254 | 01:07:25.482-01:07:25.642 | Unknown] Hmm
[u1255 | 01:07:25.642-01:07:25.642 | Unknown] ...
[u1256 | 01:07:27.102-01:07:28.442 | Seeker] it's quite an ordeal.
[u1257 | 01:07:28.442-01:07:30.202 | Seeker] I don't usually, uh,
[u1258 | 01:07:31.642-01:07:32.902 | DM] like to talk about it very much.
[u1259 | 01:07:32.902-01:07:33.582 | Kenzo] But, um,
[u1260 | 01:07:34.742-01:07:35.152 | Unknown] [laughs]
[u1261 | 01:07:36.342-01:07:43.972 | Seeker] I had a vision at that time, and, and now I, I, I journey, uh, uh, uh,
[u1262 | 01:07:46.082-01:07:50.042 | Seeker] seeking answers about a certain set of questions that I'm interested in, you might say.
[u1263 | 01:07:52.002-01:07:52.382 | Unknown] Hmm.
[u1264 | 01:07:55.342-01:07:57.122 | Kenzo] And I, I glance over at the elf.
[u1265 | 01:07:57.122-01:07:59.122 | Kenzo] Is he still in a trance?
[u1266 | 01:07:59.122-01:08:02.992 | Delwath] Well, he's looking around a little bit disturbed at the moment.
[u1267 | 01:08:02.992-01:08:03.022 | Unknown] Okay.
[u1268 | 01:08:03.022-01:08:13.042 | Delwath] Like, like there's something invisible that he's trying to find or f- or see, but, but it doesn't look like it's like a sort of very
[u1269 | 01:08:13.042-01:08:19.542 | Delwath] concentrated searching around in the area around the hyenas, but not looking at anything visible.
[u1270 | 01:08:20.942-01:08:23.962 | Kenzo] Are you still, uh, are you still, like, in a trance, or are you kind of aware?
[u1271 | 01:08:23.962-01:08:26.862 | Delwath] No, no, no. Now he's like, he looks focused-
[u1272 | 01:08:26.862-01:08:26.872 | Unknown] All right
[u1273 | 01:08:26.872-01:08:28.702 | Delwath] ... but not in a particular trance.
[u1274 | 01:08:30.642-01:08:36.642 | Delwath] After about, after about a few, a minute of that, or, or while you guys are talking, after...
[u1275 | 01:08:36.642-01:08:38.712 | Delwath] Well, you could say something at the moment.
[u1276 | 01:08:38.712-01:08:38.742 | Unknown] Yeah, yeah.
[u1277 | 01:08:38.742-01:08:40.262 | Delwath] He's like, right now he's looking around.
[u1278 | 01:08:41.362-01:08:46.122 | Kenzo] I, I, I would, I actually, uh, walk over to you, wherever you, if you're a few steps away, I just walk over.
[u1279 | 01:08:46.122-01:08:46.422 | Unknown] Yes.
[u1280 | 01:08:46.422-01:08:52.462 | Kenzo] And, uh, um, just sort of kind of motion cautiously to make sure you're not, you're not still, like...
[u1281 | 01:08:52.462-01:08:54.602 | Kenzo] You know, like I'm in, it's not, not interrupting anything.
[u1282 | 01:08:54.602-01:08:55.561 | Seeker] Right.
[u1283 | 01:08:55.562-01:08:59.842 | Kenzo] Sort of getting into your line of sight to see if you're there, if you're, you, you notice me, and sort of-
[u1284 | 01:08:59.842-01:09:01.182 | Delwath] So Delwath will look up.
[u1285 | 01:09:01.182-01:09:02.422 | DM] Yeah, he'll look up-
[u1286 | 01:09:02.422-01:09:03.182 | DM] Okay
[u1287 | 01:09:03.182-01:09:05.702 | Delwath] ... and sort of nod at you a bit and say, um,
[u1288 | 01:09:06.982-01:09:08.402 | Delwath] "Uh, apologies.
[u1289 | 01:09:08.402-01:09:09.322 | Delwath] This is a very, um,
[u1290 | 01:09:10.902-01:09:16.722 | Delwath] unexpected, although oddly not the first time it's happened here.
[u1291 | 01:09:16.722-01:09:19.982 | Delwath] And my housebility, uh, hos- hospitality to, uh,
[u1292 | 01:09:21.462-01:09:21.722 | Unknown] um,
[u1293 | 01:09:23.862-01:09:24.742 | Delwath] some, you
[u1294 | 01:09:25.922-01:09:32.382 | Delwath] very helpful warriors in this, in this battle has been, um, quite insufficient."
[u1295 | 01:09:32.382-01:09:36.502 | Delwath] And he sort of nods a bit, and, "Apologies for, for ignoring you.
[u1296 | 01:09:38.362-01:09:38.842 | DM] There's s-
[u1297 | 01:09:38.842-01:09:38.911 | Unknown] Uh
[u1298 | 01:09:38.911-01:09:41.382 | Delwath] ... something odd going on here, though."
[u1299 | 01:09:41.382-01:09:43.982 | Kenzo] All right. What's your name, traveler?
[u1300 | 01:09:43.982-01:09:44.782 | Delwath] Uh, Delwath.
[u1301 | 01:09:46.422-01:09:48.821 | Delwath] I've been here for s- for several months, actually.
[u1302 | 01:09:50.422-01:09:52.602 | Wellby] And this wasn't the first attack?
[u1303 | 01:09:52.602-01:09:53.102 | Kenzo] The third.
[u1304 | 01:09:54.202-01:09:57.582 | Seeker] Hmm. Is it always these, uh, hyena creatures?
[u1305 | 01:09:57.582-01:09:59.322 | Delwath] No, this is the f- this is the...
[u1306 | 01:09:59.322-01:10:01.102 | Delwath] Uh, hyenas, yes.
[u1307 | 01:10:01.102-01:10:03.562 | Delwath] Um, this size, no.
[u1308 | 01:10:05.482-01:10:06.622 | Seeker] Oh, they're normally smaller.
[u1309 | 01:10:08.122-01:10:10.182 | Delwath] Well, they're normally hyena-sized.
[u1310 | 01:10:10.182-01:10:10.582 | Unknown] Aha.
[u1311 | 01:10:12.342-01:10:15.582 | Seeker] Yeah, these ones look big enough you could put a saddle on.
[u1312 | 01:10:15.582-01:10:16.742 | Delwath] Yeah. And there's,
[u1313 | 01:10:17.962-01:10:20.722 | Delwath] there's something wrong with the magic here.
[u1314 | 01:10:22.662-01:10:24.682 | Seeker] Um, I perk up at this.
[u1315 | 01:10:24.682-01:10:26.302 | Seeker] [laughs] What do you mean about that?
[u1316 | 01:10:33.182-01:10:33.642 | Kenzo] Um,
[u1317 | 01:10:34.922-01:10:35.722 | DM] hold on a second.
[u1318 | 01:10:40.522-01:10:45.191 | Delwath] It, it's hard to expl- [laughs] it's hard to explain exactly.
[u1319 | 01:10:45.191-01:10:48.242 | Delwath] I can't quite see where it's coming from.
[u1320 | 01:10:51.162-01:10:52.782 | Kenzo] But there's a-
[u1321 | 01:10:52.782-01:10:52.792 | Unknown] Hmm
[u1322 | 01:10:52.792-01:10:56.982 | Delwath] ... there's like a, there's something distorted here.
[u1323 | 01:10:59.562-01:11:00.422 | Seeker] Distorted.
[u1324 | 01:11:01.662-01:11:03.762 | Kenzo] Delwath, do you know Candrosa?
[u1325 | 01:11:05.822-01:11:08.162 | Delwath] Uh, yeah. I've met her a few times.
[u1326 | 01:11:09.542-01:11:11.682 | Kenzo] Huh. A she.
[u1327 | 01:11:11.682-01:11:12.191 | Kenzo] Uh, him.
[u1328 | 01:11:12.191-01:11:12.222 | Unknown] Him.
[u1329 | 01:11:12.222-01:11:13.722 | Delwath] No, him, I think.
[u1330 | 01:11:13.722-01:11:15.322 | Delwath] Tim, I don't remember.
[u1331 | 01:11:15.322-01:11:16.452 | Delwath] Him or her.
[u1332 | 01:11:16.452-01:11:17.512 | Delwath] Uh, he. Candrosa-
[u1333 | 01:11:17.512-01:11:17.802 | Unknown] He, sorry.
[u1334 | 01:11:19.902-01:11:21.362 | Delwath] I don't have my little cheat sheet open.
[u1335 | 01:11:22.902-01:11:23.782 | Delwath] I'm also interested-
[u1336 | 01:11:23.782-01:11:25.082 | Delwath] I've met him a few times
[u1337 | 01:11:25.082-01:11:27.182 | Seeker] ... I'm also interested in meeting Candrosa.
[u1338 | 01:11:29.082-01:11:30.122 | Kenzo] Huh.
[u1339 | 01:11:30.122-01:11:30.702 | Seeker] Uh.
[u1340 | 01:11:30.702-01:11:32.262 | Kenzo] Apparently we have this in common, friend.
[u1341 | 01:11:33.582-01:11:33.962 | Unknown] Indeed.
[u1342 | 01:11:35.642-01:11:36.662 | Kenzo] Why do you seek Candrosa?
[u1343 | 01:11:38.422-01:11:44.182 | Seeker] Well, I came to this place because it's, uh, it's got all kinds of interesting features, actually.
[u1344 | 01:11:44.182-01:11:45.272 | Kenzo] Um-
[u1345 | 01:11:45.272-01:11:45.272 | Unknown] Uh
[u1346 | 01:11:45.272-01:11:55.442 | Seeker] ... if, if you, uh, look at the geology around us, um, uh, we're in this, uh, uh, desert plain, but obviously there's a sandstone, um, uh, mesa,
[u1347 | 01:11:55.442-01:11:57.962 | Seeker] uh, with, uh, quite beautiful grain and, and texture.

[u2498 | 02:16:09.188-02:16:12.568 | Delwath] I've been, um, curious about them for some time,
[u2499 | 02:16:13.808-02:16:16.468 | Delwath] but they've revealed their secrets very, um,

[u2503 | 02:16:22.527-02:16:27.588 | Seeker] While, uh, residing in that town, Delwath,

[u2518 | 02:17:35.268-02:17:35.608 | Unknown] Oh,
[u2519 | 02:17:37.068-02:17:39.808 | Seeker] I long to explore that world.
[u2520 | 02:17:39.808-02:17:45.048 | Seeker] I spent some time with the lizard folk, who have a deep connection to that area,

[u2524 | 02:18:06.848-02:18:07.128 | Unknown] um,
[u2525 | 02:18:09.008-02:18:14.928 | Delwath] sorta seems to be staring off into space a bit, and, like, he looks a little, like...
[u2526 | 02:18:16.688-02:18:22.248 | Delwath] You, you... Anybody who would be paying attention, you, you know what, Kong, you have a good insight roll.

[u2529 | 02:18:24.658-02:18:24.978 | Unknown] ... what you would notice.

[u2532 | 02:18:34.908-02:18:36.328 | Delwath] Um, I just need to think of how to phrase this.

[u2551 | 02:19:32.868-02:19:41.468 | DM] more sort of convoluted, and you occasionally see these, like, little slot canyons going off in sort of blind alleys.

[u2562 | 02:20:28.424-02:20:30.284 | DM] it's quite, um,

[u2595 | 02:22:36.084-02:22:36.414 | Unknown] Yeah. Hmm.

[u2931 | 02:41:35.796-02:41:37.636 | DM] "You know, but the, the

[u2938 | 02:42:06.676-02:42:14.616 | DM] but if what you said is true and danger is coming out of the east again, I fear that, that, um, you know, maybe those days of my childhood are coming back.

[u2943 | 02:42:28.616-02:42:38.716 | DM] You're now... I mean, you're on this ancient, this sort of old trade road where you can see occasionally, like, um, it's mostly just a track kind
[u2944 | 02:42:38.716-02:42:48.896 | DM] of kicked up through the, you know, s- through the sand, but every now and then there's, like, a paving stone or a, um, you know, kind of a, a little bit of ancient history

[u2946 | 02:42:52.116-02:42:53.256 | DM] uh, you know, uh...
[u2947 | 02:42:56.766-02:43:00.596 | DM] Uh, you can sort of, you can sense, you can sort of see the history of the road.
[u2948 | 02:43:00.596-02:43:05.036 | DM] Like, you can tell that this used to be, many, many years ago, well-traveled.

[u2968 | 02:44:22.316-02:44:25.315 | Kenzo] Well, we seek the origins of these strange hyenas.

[u2972 | 02:44:34.056-02:44:39.536 | DM] I haven't seen anything related to those, but strange beasts, I always say, must be the Blasted Lands.

[u2987 | 02:45:42.116-02:45:46.976 | DM] people coming in, gathering at Gomat and heading into Karawa for the,

[u3054 | 02:49:22.436-02:49:23.326 | DM] Yeah. I, I don't know-
[u3055 | 02:49:23.326-02:49:26.996 | DM] Uh, they both happened, they both happened within the past two weeks.

[u3072 | 02:50:26.476-02:50:36.176 | Kenzo] Well, if we, if we make haste to the oasis, learn what, learn what we can there, uh, we can be back in, back to town in, what, two days, three days?

[u3086 | 02:51:32.108-02:51:33.708 | Kenzo] "Friend, what's your name?

[u3088 | 02:51:36.368-02:51:37.847 | Kenzo] It's good to seem, see you here."

[u3092 | 02:51:45.628-02:51:48.628 | Kenzo] my companions and I, I think we're gonna head to the oasis.

[u3095 | 02:51:52.288-02:51:55.048 | Kenzo] careful. These are, these are dangerous times.
